Algorithm LP-iterate:
- Choose n (maybe 512 or 1024). Initialize p with an asymmetric shape (e.g., low-dimensional basis, or parent? We can ignore parent).
- Loop while time:
  - c = conv(p,p); M = max(c)
  - Build A: for k 0..2n-2, A[k,i]=p_{k-i} if 0<=k-i<n else 0.
  - Solve LP: maximize cvec = ones(n) dot y subject to A y <= M, y>=0.
  - If solver success and sum_y>0, normalize b = y / sum(y).
  - New p = (1-t)*p + t*b (or maybe p_new normalized). Evaluate score. Keep best.
  - Step t: start small 0.2; if score improves, maybe keep; else reduce. The parent says update f ← (1-t)f + t b. It preserves nonnegative. It normalizes to fixed sum? b normalized, p normalized, convex combo normalized.
- Use multiple initializations.

Implement:
def p_w_grad(p, w):
    n=len(p)
    L=1 << (3*n-2).bit_length()
    W=fft(w,L)
    Pr=fft(p[::-1],L)
    C=ifft(W*Pr).real
    return C[n-1:n-1+n]
This gives g1 = ∑ w_k p_{k-j}. Then gradient of smooth sum is 2*g1 (no diagonal factor because w aggregated? Let's verify: For objective S=∑ w_k c_k, ∂c_k/∂p_j=2 p_{k-j} if valid. So ∂S/∂p_j=2∑ w_k p_{k-j}. So g=2*p_w_grad.
Good.

Local gradient routine:
```
def local_refine(p, iters_limit, deadline, q_start=8, q_end=32):
    n=p.size
    best=p.copy(); best_score=score_fft(p)
    v=np.zeros(n)
    alpha=1.0/n
    for it in range(iters):
       if time>deadline: break
       c=conv_self_fft(p); m=c.max()
       # adjust q
       q = q_start + (q_end-q_start)*it/iters
       logw = q*np.log(np.maximum(c,1e-300))
       logw -= logw.max()
       w = np.exp(np.clip(logw,-700,0)); w/=w.sum()
       g = 2*corr_p_w(p,w)
       gmax = np.max(np.abs(g))
       if gmax<1e-18: break
       d = -g/gmax
       v = 0.85*v + 0.15*d
       # normalize v max? d max1, v max1
       step = alpha * (1.0 + 0.5*np.sin? no)
       p_new = p + step * v
       p_new = np.maximum(p_new, 1e-12)
       p_new += 1e-6  # floor? maybe not
       p_new = p_new / p_new.sum()
       # optional mix uniform
       p_new = 0.9995*p_new + 0.0005/n
       if score_fft(p_new) < best_score:
           best_score; best=p_new.copy()
       # If not improve, maybe step smaller? We can track.
       p = p_new  # or keep? Use p_new anyway
    return best,best_score
```
But if p_new score worse, continuing from it may drift away. We can use simulated annealing: accept if better or random < T. Or always use p_new but keep best. With normalized gradient, maybe p worsens then recovers. To avoid bad, use backtracking: try step alphas [step, step/2, step/4] and accept first improving; else accept smallest with minimal worsening if score not too bad. This costs extra score evals. We can simplify: use small step, e.g., alpha=0.2/n, and update p_new. It may monotonically improve? Not guaranteed. We can evaluate and only set p=p_new if score_new < current_score or it%10==0 (accept). Keep current p if not. This prevents drift. If stuck, add random perturbation.

Pseudo:
cur_score=score(p)
for it:
 compute g from p
 d = -g/gmax
 v=...
 for factor in [1,0.5,0.25]:
   p_try=p + alpha*factor*v
   p_try=clip normalize
   s_try=score_fft(p_try)
   if s_try < cur_score:
      p,p_score=p_try,s_try; break
 else:
   # no improvement: try small random perturbation around best every 200 iterations
   if it%200==0:
      p_try = best + 0.01*rand; normalize; if improve? etc.
```

LP improve function:
```
def lp_refine(p, deadline, max_solves=200, init_alpha=0.2):
    n=p.size
    best=p.copy(); best_score=score_fft(p)
    current=p.copy(); current_score=best_score
    for it in range(max_solves):
       if now>deadline: break
       c=self_conv(current); M=c.max()
       if not finite or M<=0: break
       try build A
       res=linprog(...)
       if not success: break
       b=res.x
       if b is None or sum_b<=0: break
       b_norm=b/sum_b
       # Try t list based on current speed
       best_t_score=inf
       for t in [0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5,0.7]:
           p_try=(1-t)*current + t*b_norm
           p_try=as_p(p_try)
           s=score_fft(p_try)
           if s<best_t_score: ...
       if best_t_score < current_score - 1e-12:
           current=best_t_try; current_score=best_t_score
           if current_score<best_score: best=current.copy(); best_score=current_score
       else:
           # try t close 1? or break if small
           # If no improvement, maybe use b as current? If score(b) < current: accept else break.
    return best,best_score
```
Because p current normalized; b_norm normalized; t convex preserves sum. We also can try b alone (t=1) if better.
Potential issue: if p current has small M, LP b may be highly constrained; if t small, little change. Good.

Now, local_refine details. We need avoid too much code but okay.
```
def local_refine(p0, deadline, q0=6, q1=40):
    n=p0.size
    p = as_p(p0)
    cur_c = self_conv(p); cur_m = cur_c.max(); cur_score=2*n*cur_m; best=p.copy(); best_score=cur_score
    v = np.zeros(n)
    base_alpha = 2.0/n
    no_improve=0
    it=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       it+=1
       beta = q0 + (q1-q0)*min(1.0, it/500)
       c=cur_c
       # weights
       logw = beta * np.log(np.maximum(c, 1e-300))
       logw -= logw.max()
       w = np.exp(np.clip(logw, -700, 0)); sw=w.sum(); if sw<1: w=onehot argmax; else w/=sw
       g=2*cross_p_w(p,w)
       gmax=np.max(np.abs(g))
       if gmax<1e-18: break
       d = -g/gmax
       v = 0.8*v + 0.2*d
       vmax=np.max(np.abs(v)); if vmax<1e-12: break
       v = v/vmax
       # step anneal
       alpha = base_alpha * max(0.01, 1.0 - it/2000)
       improved=False
       for factor in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25):
           p_try = p + alpha*factor*v
           p_try = np.maximum(p_try, 1e-12)
           p_try = p_try / p_try.sum()
           # mix slight uniform to avoid zero
           p_try = 0.999*p_try + 0.001/n
           s_try = score_fft(p_try)
           if s_try < cur_score:
               p=p_try; cur_c=self_conv(p); cur_m=cur_c.max(); cur_score=s_try
               if cur_score<best_score: best=p.copy(); best_score=cur_score
               no_improve=3
               improved=True
               break
           no_improve += 1
       if not improved:
           no_improve += 3
           if no_improve > 100:
               # restart around best with noise
               noise = 0.05/rng? Need rng. We can pass rng.
               p_try = best + 0.02*rng.standard_normal(n)
               p_try = np.maximum(p_try,1e-12); p_try/=p_try.sum()
               s_try=score_fft(p_try)
               if s_try < cur_score: ...
               else: # accept anyway maybe
                   p=p_try; cur...
               no_improve=0
               v.fill(0)
    return best,best_score```

def central_binom(n):
 a=np.empty(n); a[0]=1; for i in range(n-1): a[i+1]=a[i]*(2*i+1)/(2*i+2); return a/a.sum()

```
import numpy as np, time, math
from scipy.fft import fft, ifft
from scipy.optimize import linprog

def _fftnext(m): return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()

def _self_conv(a):
 n=len(a); m=2*n-1; L=1 << (m-1).bit_length()
 A=fft(a,L); C=ifft(A*A); return C.real[:m]
```
Careful `1 << (m-1).bit_length()` for m=1? If m=1, (0).bit_length()=0, 1<<0=1 okay. For m=2n-1>1.

 n=len(a); c=_self_conv(a); c=np.maximum(c,0); m=c.max(); if m==0 return inf; if sum? assume normalized. return 2.0*n*m. If a sum not 1, evaluator scale; but internal normalize.

`_as_p(a)`:
 arr=np.asarray(a,float); arr=np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0, posinf=1000, neginf=-1000); arr=np.clip(arr,0,1000); s=arr.sum(); if s<1e-12: arr=np.ones_like(arr)/len; s=1 else arr/=s; return arr.

`lp_refine` should use internal scores; to avoid too many exact, internal.
```
def lp_refine(p0, deadline, rng, max_solves=200):
 p=as_p(p0); best=p.copy(); best_score=_score_fft(p); current=p; cur_score=best_score
 n=p.size; onearg = np.ones(n)
 for it in range(max_solves):
   if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
   c=_self_conv(current); M=c.max()
   if M<=0 or not finite: break
   A=np.zeros((2*n-1,n))
   # vectorized build maybe using indices:
   rows = np.repeat(np.arange(n), n)
   cols = np.tile(np.arange(n), n)
   A[rows + cols? Wait for each col i, rows i..i+n-1. We can build with loops easier.
   for i in range(n): A[i:i+n, i]=current[i]
   bounds=[(0,10.0)]*n
   try:
      res=linprog(c=-onearg, A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(2*n-1,M), bounds=bounds, method='HiGHS', options={'time_limit': max(1, deadline-time.monotonic()-2)})
   except Exception:
      try res=linprog(... method='highs')
   if not res.success: break
   b=res.x
   sb=b.sum()
   if sb<=1e-12: break
   b=b/sb
   # try t
   best_try=None
   for t in (0.05,0.1,0.2,0.35,0.5,0.7,1.0):
      p_try=(1-t)*current + t*b
      p_try=as_p(p_try)
      st=_score_fft(p_try)
      if st<best... 
   if best_t_score < cur_score - 1e-12:
       current=best_try; cur_score; update best
   else:
       # try a few random scales? if no improvement, maybe accept b if much better than current? best t includes 1.0. Break.
       break
 return best,best_score
```

   mx=logw.max(); logw-=mx

   else w/=sw
   g=2*_cross(p,w)
   gmax=np.max(np.abs(g))
   if not finite or gmax<1e-18: break
   d=-g/gmax
   v=0.8*v + 0.2*d
   vmax=np.max(np.abs(v)); if vmax<1e-12: v=d; vmax=1; else v=vmax? v/=vmax
   alpha=base_alpha * max(0.05, 1.0 - it/5000)
   accepted=False
   for factor in (1.0,0.5,0.25):

      p_try=np.maximum(p_try,1e-12)
      p_try[:]+ 1e-9? then normalize

   if not accepted:
      no_improve +=1
      if no_improve > 60:

         p_try = best + rng.normal(0, max(1e-4, 0.05/n), n) # absolute 0.0001

But we can just run minimal. We'll include simple if total < 120: evaluate parent and base n=128 and return best. This ensures quick.

Need parent list creation:
```
parent_list = [float(x) for x in np.clip(np.asarray(height_sequence_1),0,1000)] if available else [1.0,1.0]
parent_exact = float(evaluate_sequence(parent_list))
```
Computing parent_exact with length 4498 maybe 0.05s, fine.
If parent invalid (score inf), set parent_exact=2.0 and parent_list=[1.0,1.0].

For fallback if parent_exact invalid, base.

Code for auto proposals uses `_score_c` for candidate p sum1. Good.

Let's write final program now. Ensure no line after fence. Use only allowed dependencies. final should be one Python fenced block.
</think>

```python
import numpy as np
import time

from scipy.fft import fft, ifft
from scipy.optimize import linprog


def _next_pow2_threadsafe(m: int) -> int:
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def _as_probability(a) -> np.ndarray:
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=-1000.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(arr))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-15:
        arr = np.ones(arr.size, dtype=np.float64)
        arr /= arr.size
    else:
        arr = arr / s
    return arr


def _self_conv(a: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    n = int(a.size)
    out_len = 2 * n - 1
    L = _next_pow2_threadsafe(out_len)
    A = fft(a, L)
    C = ifft(A * A)
    C = C.real[:out_len]
    return np.maximum(C, 0.0)


def _score_c(a: np.ndarray):
    c = _self_conv(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf"), c
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 0.0:
        return float("inf"), c
    return float(2.0 * a.size * m), c


def _score(a: np.ndarray) -> float:
    return _score_c(a)[0]


def _cross_gradient(p: np.ndarray, w: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    n = int(p.size)
    out_len = n + int(w.size) - 1
    L = _next_pow2_threadsafe(out_len)
    W = fft(w, L)
    Pr = fft(p[::-1], L)
    c = ifft(W * Pr).real
    return c[n - 1:n - 1 + n]


def _central_binom(n: int) -> np.ndarray:
    n = int(max(2, n))
    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[0] = 1.0
    for i in range(n - 1):
        a[i + 1] = a[i] * (2 * i + 1) / (2 * i + 2)
    return _as_probability(a)


def _add_candidate(cands, p, n):
    if p is None:
        return
    p = _as_probability(p)
    if p.size != int(n) or not np.isfinite(p).all():
        return
    s = _score(p)
    if np.isfinite(s):
        cands.append((s, p.copy()))


def _unique_top(cands, k):
    cands = [x for x in cands if np.isfinite(x[0])]
    if not cands:
        return []
    cands.sort(key=lambda z: z[0])
    out = []
    seen = []
    for s, p in cands:
        sig = (float(p[0]), float(np.clip(p.size, 1, 9999)), float(np.sum(p * p)))
        same = any(
            abs(sig[0] - q[0]) <= 1e-10 and
            abs(sig[1] - q[1]) <= 1e-8 and
            abs(sig[2] - q[2]) <= 1e-10
            for q in seen
        )
        if same:
            continue
        seen.append(sig)
        out.append((s, p.copy()))
        if len(out) >= int(k):
            break
    return out


def _generate_candidates(n: int, rng) -> list:
    cands = []
    n = int(n)
    t = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)

    base = _central_binom(n)
    _add_candidate(cands, base, n)

    basis = []
    basis.append(2.0 * t - 1.0)

    for m in range(1, 17):
        basis.append(np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * m * t))
    for m in range(1, 10):
        basis.append(np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * m * t + 0.25))
        basis.append(np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * m * t + 0.75))

    for B in (4, 8, 16, 32):
        for _ in range(3):
            vals = rng.normal(size=B)
            centers = (np.arange(B, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(B)
            basis.append(np.interp(t, centers, vals))

    for _ in range(24):
        K = int(rng.integers(3, 9))
        freqs = rng.integers(1, 17, size=K)
        phases = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi, size=K)
        weights = rng.normal(size=K)
        u = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for f, ph, wgt in zip(freqs, phases, weights):
            u += wgt * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * float(f) * t + ph)
        basis.append(u)

    for c0 in (0.12, 0.25, 0.40, 0.60, 0.75, 0.88):
        for sg in (0.04, 0.08, 0.14, 0.22):
            basis.append(np.exp(-((t - c0) * (t - c0)) / (2.0 * sg * sg)))

    # Delayed-scaled base factors, reminiscent of multiplying by (1 +/- lambda x^m).
    for mshift in (6, 12, 24, 48, 96, 192):
        if mshift >= n:
            continue
        for lam in (0.1, 0.25, 0.5, 0.8, 1.1):
            p_plus = base.copy()
            p_plus[mshift:] += lam * base[:n - mshift]
            _add_candidate(cands, p_plus, n)

            p_minus = base.copy()
            p_minus[mshift:] -= lam * base[:n - mshift]
            _add_candidate(cands, p_minus, n)

    # Multiplicative perturbations of the central-binomial seed.
    for u0 in basis:
        u = np.asarray(u0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        if u.size != n:
            continue
        u -= float(np.mean(u))
        amax = float(np.max(np.abs(u)))
        if not np.isfinite(amax) or amax < 1e-15:
            continue
        u /= amax
        for rho in (0.10, 0.25, 0.50, 0.80, 1.00):
            p = base * (1.0 + rho * u)
            _add_candidate(cands, p, n)

    # Direct low-dimensional smooth constructions.
    for rho in (0.3, 0.6, 0.9):
        p = 1.0 + rho * (2.0 * t - 1.0)
        _add_candidate(cands, p, n)

    # Anti-symmetric bounded random signs.
    half = n // 2
    for rho in (0.3, 0.6, 0.95):
        for _ in range(4):
            s = rng.choice(np.array([-1.0, 1.0], dtype=np.float64), size=n)
            s[half:] = -s[:half][::-1]
            if n % 2:
                s[half] = 0.0
            p = 1.0 + rho * s
            _add_candidate(cands, p, n)

    # Plain random softmax starts.
    for _ in range(12):
        z = rng.normal(size=n)
        z -= np.max(z)
        p = np.exp(z)
        _add_candidate(cands, p, n)

    return cands


def _local_refine(p0: np.ndarray, deadline: float, rng, q0: float = 6.0, q1: float = 80.0):
    p = _as_probability(p0)
    n = int(p.size)
    best = p.copy()
    best_score, best_c = _score_c(best)

    cur_score, cur_c = _score_c(p)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    base_alpha = 1.0 / float(n)
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        beta = q0 + (q1 - q0) * min(1.0, float(it) / 2500.0)

        c = cur_c
        if c.size == 0:
            break

        safe = np.maximum(c, 1e-300)
        logw = beta * np.log(safe)
        logw -= float(np.max(logw))
        w = np.exp(np.clip(logw, -700.0, 0.0))
        sw = float(np.sum(w))
        if not np.isfinite(sw) or sw <= 1e-15:
            w = np.zeros_like(c)
            w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        else:
            w /= sw

        g = 2.0 * _cross_gradient(p, w)
        gmax = float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
        if not np.isfinite(gmax) or gmax <= 1e-18:
            break

        d = -g / gmax
        v = 0.82 * v + 0.18 * d
        vmax = float(np.max(np.abs(v)))
        if not np.isfinite(vmax) or vmax <= 1e-14:
            v = d.copy()
            vmax = 1.0
        else:
            v /= vmax

        alpha = base_alpha * max(0.04, 1.0 - float(it) / 6000.0)
        accepted = False

        for factor in (1.0, 0.55, 0.25):
            p_try = p + alpha * factor * v
            p_try = np.maximum(p_try, 1e-12)
            s_try = float(np.sum(p_try))
            if not np.isfinite(s_try) or s_try <= 0.0:
                continue
            p_try /= s_try
            p_try = 0.9999 * p_try + 1e-6 / float(n)
            sc_try, c_try = _score_c(p_try)
            if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try < cur_score:
                p = p_try
                cur_score = sc_try
                cur_c = c_try
                if cur_score < best_score:
                    best = p.copy()
                    best_score = cur_score
                accepted = True
                no_improve = 0
                break

        if not accepted:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 90:
                noise_scale = max(1e-6, 0.18 / float(n))
                p_try = best + rng.normal(0.0, noise_scale, size=n)
                p_try = np.maximum(p_try, 1e-12)
                s_try = float(np.sum(p_try))
                if s_try > 0.0:
                    p_try /= s_try
                    sc_try, c_try = _score_c(p_try)
                    p = p_try
                    cur_score = sc_try if np.isfinite(sc_try) else best_score
                    cur_c = c_try if c_try.size else _self_conv(p)
                no_improve = 0
                v[:] = 0.0

    return best, best_score


def _lp_refine(p0: np.ndarray, deadline: float, rng):
    p = _as_probability(p0)
    n = int(p.size)
    best = p.copy()
    best_score, _ = _score_c(best)

    cur = p.copy()
    cur_score, _ = _score_c(cur)
    obj = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    bounds = [(0.0, 10.0) for _ in range(n)]

    for _ in range(2000):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break

        c = _self_conv(cur)
        M = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
            break

        A = np.zeros((2 * n - 1, n), dtype=np.float64)
        for i in range(n):
            A[i:i + n, i] = cur[i]
        bvec = np.full(2 * n - 1, M, dtype=np.float64)

        remaining = max(0.5, deadline - time.monotonic())
        opts = {"time_limit": float(min(18.0, remaining * 0.45))}

        res = None
        try:
            res = linprog(c=obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=bvec, bounds=bounds,
                          method="HiGHS", options=opts)
        except Exception:
            try:
                res = linprog(c=obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=bvec, bounds=bounds,
                              method="highs")
            except Exception:
                try:
                    res = linprog(c=obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=bvec, bounds=bounds,
                                  method="interior-point")
                except Exception:
                    break

        if res is None or not getattr(res, "success", False):
            break
        b = np.asarray(getattr(res, "x", None), dtype=np.float64)
        if b is None or b.size != n:
            break
        b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
        sb = float(np.sum(b))
        if not np.isfinite(sb) or sb <= 1e-14:
            break
        b = b / sb

        chosen = None
        chosen_score = cur_score
        for tt in (0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.35, 0.55, 0.80, 1.00):
            p_try = (1.0 - tt) * cur + tt * b
            p_try = _as_probability(p_try)
            sc, _ = _score_c(p_try)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < chosen_score:
                chosen = p_try
                chosen_score = sc

        if chosen is None or chosen_score >= cur_score - 1e-13:
            break

        cur = chosen.copy()
        cur_score = chosen_score
        if cur_score < best_score:
            best = cur.copy()
            best_score = cur_score

    return best, best_score


def _exact_score(p: np.ndarray) -> float:
    try:
        return float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)]))
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget = float(max(30.0, budget_s))
    hard_deadline = t0 + max(20.0, budget - 25.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    parent_list = None
    parent_exact = float("inf")
    try:
        parent_arr = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if parent_arr.size > 0 and np.isfinite(parent_arr).all():
            parent_arr = np.clip(parent_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
            parent_list = [float(x) for x in parent_arr]
            parent_exact = float(evaluate_sequence(parent_list))
    except Exception:
        parent_list = None
        parent_exact = float("inf")

    if parent_list is None or not np.isfinite(parent_exact):
        parent_list = [1.0, 1.0]
        parent_exact = 2.0

    # Very small budgets: return a compact good construction or parent.
    if budget < 90.0:
        for n in (128, 256, 512):
            p = _central_binom(n)
            sc = _exact_score(p)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < parent_exact:
                return [float(x) for x in np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)]
        return parent_list

    # Main optimization resolution. 512 is a good balance for dense LPs and FFT.
    N = 512

    cands = _generate_candidates(N, rng)
    if not cands:
        cands = [( _score(_central_binom(N)), _central_binom(N) )]

    top = _unique_top(cands, 12)
    if not top:
        fb = _central_binom(N)
        top = [(_score(fb), fb)]

    # Phase 1: local smooth-minimax polishing from the best seeds.
    local_deadline = t0 + min(0.52 * budget, hard_deadline - 260.0)
    polish_pool = []
    for idx, (sc, p) in enumerate(top):
        if time.monotonic() >= local_deadline - 15.0:
            break
        remaining_tasks = max(1, len(top) - idx)
        chunk = (local_deadline - time.monotonic()) / float(remaining_tasks)
        chunk = max(6.0, min(180.0, chunk))
        p2, s2 = _local_refine(p, time.monotonic() + chunk, rng, q0=4.0, q1=90.0)
        polish_pool.append((s2, p2))

    # Keep a good second-phase pool: locally improved plus original top seeds.
    second_cands = []
    for sc, p in polish_pool:
        second_cands.append((sc, p))
    for sc, p in top:
        second_cands.append((sc, p))
    top2 = _unique_top(second_cands, 10)

    # Phase 2: exact LP directions on the strongest candidates.
    lp_deadline = t0 + min(0.82 * budget, hard_deadline - 140.0)
    lp_pool = []
    for idx, (sc, p) in enumerate(top2):
        if time.monotonic() >= lp_deadline - 20.0:
            break
        remaining_tasks = max(1, len(top2) - idx)
        chunk = (lp_deadline - time.monotonic()) / float(remaining_tasks)
        chunk = max(8.0, min(180.0, chunk))
        p2, s2 = _lp_refine(p, time.monotonic() + chunk, rng)
        lp_pool.append((s2, p2))

    combined = []
    for sc, p in polish_pool:
        combined.append((sc, p))
    for sc, p in lp_pool:
        combined.append((sc, p))
    for sc, p in top:
        combined.append((sc, p))

    best_opt, best_internal = _unique_top(combined, 1)[0]

    # Final concise polishing on the global best candidate.
    final_local_deadline = min(hard_deadline - 70.0, time.monotonic() + max(30.0, 0.18 * budget))
    if time.monotonic() < final_local_deadline - 5.0:
        q0 = max(20.0, 0.5 * best_internal * N / (N + 1.0) + 1.0)
        p3, s3 = _local_refine(best_opt, final_local_deadline, rng, q0=q0, q1=180.0)
        if np.isfinite(s3) and s3 < best_internal:
            best_opt, best_internal = p3, s3

    final_lp_deadline = min(hard_deadline - 25.0, time.monotonic() + max(15.0, 0.20 * budget))
    if time.monotonic() < final_lp_deadline - 5.0:
        p4, s4 = _lp_refine(best_opt, final_lp_deadline, rng)
        if np.isfinite(s4) and s4 < best_internal:
            best_opt, best_internal = p4, s4

    best_exact = _exact_score(best_opt)

    # Safety: exact validation and fallback.
    if not np.isfinite(best_exact):
        return parent_list
    if np.isfinite(parent_exact) and parent_exact <= best_exact:
        return parent_list

    best_list = [float(x) for x in np.clip(best_opt, 0.0, 1000.0)]
    try:
        if len(best_list) == 0 or not np.isfinite(float(evaluate_sequence(best_list))):
            return parent_list
    except Exception:
        return parent_list

    return best_list
```
